a paraperigonium or paracorolla."],"topics":["biology","botany","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["Any appendage of an organism that resembles a crown or corona (sense 4.1).","An annular ciliated organ on the head of rotifers, used for locomotion and sweeping food into the mouth."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"glosses":["Any appendage of an organism that resembles a crown or corona (sense 4.1).","The main body of the test of an echinoid, consisting of ambulacral and interambulacral areas."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"glosses":["Any appendage of an organism that resembles a crown or corona (sense 4.1).","The crown of a crinoid, consisting of a cuplike central body (theca) and a set of arms."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","zoology"]},{"glosses":["Any appendage of an organism that resembles a crown or corona (sense 4.1).","A fringe of large, bulbous surface projections on coronaviruses, formed by viral spike peplomers, creating an appearance reminiscent of the solar corona."],"topics":["biology","microbiology","natural-sciences","virology"]},{"glosses":["A luminous appearance caused by corona discharge, often seen as a bluish glow in the air adjacent to pointed metal conductors carrying high voltages."],"topics":["business","electrical-engineering","electricity","electromagnetism","energy","engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]},{"glosses":["A circle or set of circles visible around a bright celestial object, especially the Sun or the Moon, attributable to an optical phenomenon produced by the diffraction of its light by small water droplets or tiny ice crystals."],"topics":["climatology","meteorology","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["A mineral zone, consisting of one or more minerals, which surrounds another mineral or lies at the interface of two minerals, typically in a radial arrangement;
